🎯

核心关注（ML × ferro / 凝聚态）

8 篇

本日进展集中在机器学习势的可信度与动力学稳定性：BNN、deep ensemble、SchNet、PaiNN、NequIP、Allegro、MACE等模型被置于不确定性和长时间分子动力学稳定性的统一检验下。半导体缺陷预筛选和PINN振动稳定性预测把DFT数据压缩为可扩展的候选筛查器，覆盖CdSe纳米晶及一般无机半导体。磁性方向则推进了二维半导体中的动量空间共振手性反铁磁、腔真空驱动量子霍尔铁磁和自旋液体模型，但这些工作主要依赖解析理论或多体数值，并非直接的ML材料模拟。对团队而言，equivariant GNN、MACE、NEP与DFT+U可优先迁移到NbOI2、CrI3、CrSBr和BaTiO3的极化翻转、自旋-晶格动力学及缺陷筛选。

  1. 01
    文献研究：Bayesian neural networks versus deep ensembles for uncertainty quantification in machine learning interatomic potentials
    Bayesian neural networks versus deep ensembles for uncertainty quantification in machine learning interatomic potentials
    🎯 核心关注📖 Machine Learning: Science and Technology

    💡 亮点：本文比较变分贝叶斯神经网络与深度集成方法在机器学习原子间势中的不确定性量化能力，并在TiO2结构和QM7分子数据集上评估其预测可靠性与泛化能力。研究重点是识别分布外结构、估计模型置信度，并提高机器学习势在原子模拟中的安全使用能力。

    📐 方法要点：文章比较贝叶斯神经网络与深度集成在机器学习原子间势不确定性量化中的表现。两类模型都以原子结构为输入、以总能和原子力为监督目标；BNN通过权重后验或变分近似传播参数不确定性，deep ensemble则训练多个不同初始化、数据扰动或小批次顺序的独立势函数，再由预测均值和方差估计认知不确定性。所给摘要未披露具体网络、数据规模、先验、集成数和校准指标，复现时必须记录这些设置，并分别检验能量、力及外推构型上的覆盖率。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该工作直接呼应团队发展的机器学习势、主动学习和等变图神经网络，核心差别是把力场精度评价推进到置信度可靠性，而不是只报告测试集MAE。BNN适合表达参数后验，但大模型变分推断成本高；deep ensemble易并行、工程稳定，却可能低估共同数据偏差。对Hongyu Yu和Hongjun Xiang的ML势及ML Kohn-Sham哈密顿量尤其相关，也连接Xin-Gao Gong在缺陷、非绝热动力学中的主动学习需求；Weibin Chu的长时间载流子动力学同样需要这种失效预警。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可在NbOI2、BaTiO3和CrSBr上建立DFT或DFT+U构型池，覆盖平衡态、极化翻转路径、畴壁、应变、自旋旋转和高温MD，再训练等变BNN与MACE deep ensemble。用能量/力误差、预测区间覆盖率、负对数似然和MD能量漂移联合筛选构型；对高不确定度结构回算DFT并迭代主动学习。最终以NEP或高精度MACE长时MD复核相变温度、畴壁迁移和自旋-晶格耦合，并比较不同不确定性指标对灾难性外推的提前预警能力。

  2. 02
    文献研究：Benchmarking Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials for Dynamical Stability in Inorganic Semiconductor Nanocrystals: A CdSe Case Study
    Benchmarking Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials for Dynamical Stability in Inorganic Semiconductor Nanocrystals: A CdSe Case Study
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：论文系统比较SchNet、PaiNN、NequIP、Allegro和MACE五种图神经网络机器学习原子间势在氯钝化CdSe纳米晶中的力精度、效率、不确定性和长时间分子动力学稳定性。研究发现，静态验证误差排名不能可靠预测动力学稳定性，NequIP具有较好的初始稳定性，而MACE需要结合集成模型主动学习补充数据后才能实现稳定模拟。

    📐 方法要点：该研究以CdSe无机半导体纳米晶为案例，比较SchNet、PaiNN、NequIP、Allegro和MACE五种图神经网络原子间势，重点不再是留出集误差，而是分子动力学中的动力学稳定性。输入应包括原子种类、三维坐标和局域邻接关系，模型输出总能并通过坐标梯度得到力；标量SchNet、带方向信息的PaiNN以及E(3)等变NequIP、Allegro、MACE形成逐步增强的几何表达。摘要未给出截断半径、层数、数据划分、时间步长和温度范围，这些参数是判断结论可迁移性的关键。

    🔗 相关工作关联：研究回应了团队长期使用ML势进行大尺度分子动力学时的核心风险：低测试误差并不保证轨迹稳定、能量守恒和结构物理性。PaiNN、NequIP、Allegro、MACE与团队的等变GNN路线一致，而SchNet可作为不含显式等变方向基线；与传统NEP相比，图网络通常更易表达复杂局域环境，但训练和推理成本可能更高。该CdSe纳米晶问题也连接Ji-Hui Yang的二维/纳米半导体、Xin-Gao Gong的缺陷动力学以及Weibin Chu的长时间非绝热模拟。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可把同一基准迁移到含表面配体、Cd空位、Se空位和应变的CdSe或钙钛矿纳米晶，并扩展到CrI3、CrSBr薄片的热稳定性。训练集除随机扰动和DFT平衡构型外，应加入高温轨迹、表面重构、缺陷扩散和不同自旋构型；磁性体系需使用DFT+U并显式标注自旋。用短轨迹外推测试、长时NVE能量漂移、径向分布函数、配位数、键长分布和相变温度验证，比较MACE/Allegro与NEP在计算成本和稳定性上的取舍。

    阅读原文 ↗
  3. 03
    文献研究：Prescreening Point Defects in Semiconductors With Machine Learning
    Prescreening Point Defects in Semiconductors With Machine Learning
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：本文构建物理引导的机器学习模型，用于高通量预筛选半导体点缺陷的形成能和零声子线，目标是避免传统缺陷结构弛豫带来的计算开销。研究以4H-SiC中的空位和替位缺陷为例，比较岭回归、核岭回归和多层感知机模型，获得了具有实用潜力的预测精度。

    📐 方法要点：文章研究用物理引导机器学习对半导体点缺陷进行预筛选，目标是在高通量DFT之前预测缺陷相关性质。合理的路线是以宿主结构、缺陷元素、空位或替位类型、电荷态、局域配位和化学势条件构造特征，输出形成能、跃迁能级、带隙内位置或缺陷稳定性；模型应通过物理约束、对称性编码、能量参考和化学空间划分减少数据偏差。所给摘要没有提供具体模型、标签、数据集规模和物理损失形式，不能据此断言采用了某一特定GNN或数值精度。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该方向与团队在Si、SiC、GaN、SiO2、WSe2、CrI3和卤化物钙钛矿中的缺陷形成能、电荷修正、深能级及非辐射复合研究高度一致。它区别于直接训练缺陷性质回归器的地方，在于先做候选压缩，再把昂贵DFT集中于高价值构型；但若忽略电荷态、费米能级、化学势和有限尺寸修正，模型容易学习到错误相关性。Xin-Gao Gong、Ji-Hui Yang的缺陷与输运工作可提供标签体系，Hongyu Yu和Hongjun Xiang可贡献物理约束GNN及张量性质模型。

    💡 对你方向的启示：建议先整理SiC色心、GaN空位、WSe2硫属空位、CrI3带电缺陷和钙钛矿反位缺陷数据库，统一超胞尺寸、化学势窗口、DFT泛函、DFT+U参数和电荷修正协议。用分层交叉验证按化学元素和缺陷类型留出，避免同一宿主的相似构型泄漏；模型输出候选排序及不确定度，再对前若干百分比进行自洽DFT、GW或BSE校验。最终把形成能预筛选与非辐射复合矩阵元、缺陷扩散NEB和载流子寿命连接起来，验证筛选是否真正改善器件相关指标。

    阅读原文 ↗
  4. 04
    文献研究：Chiral Antiferromagnetism from Momentum-Space Resonance in a 2D Semiconductor
    Chiral Antiferromagnetism from Momentum-Space Resonance in a 2D Semiconductor
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：研究二维范德华半导体 GdGaI 中由动量空间共振驱动的手性三重波矢反铁磁态，并分析其电子空穴能带边重构。结果表明，该磁性态可产生显著的动量空间 Berry 曲率，并可能带来新型输运与拓扑响应。

    📐 方法要点：该工作提出二维半导体中由动量空间共振产生手性反铁磁性的机制，重点是电子能带、磁序与晶格或轨道自由度在特定动量处发生共振耦合。其技术路线更接近对称性分析、有效哈密顿量和多体基态计算，而不是机器学习势或GNN；摘要未说明具体材料名称、交换参数、SOC处理、计算方法和模型尺寸，因此不能补写未给出的训练集或网络结构。可复现研究至少需要明确二维晶格、磁性轨道、反铁磁序、共振波矢、能隙和手性序参量的定义。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与Hongjun Xiang关注的非共线磁性、拓扑磁态、交替磁性和群论分析直接相连，也可与Hongyu Yu关于自旋哈密顿量和自旋-晶格耦合的工作衔接。不同于传统费米面不稳定性驱动的手性磁序，该机制发生在半导体动量空间共振环境，可能无需金属费米面；与CrI3、CrSBr等范德华磁体相比，关键比较对象是层间堆垛、SOC、交换各向异性和载流子浓度。当前摘要没有证据表明使用ML，因此应把它视为可被ML加速的物理机制候选。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可针对CrI3、CrSBr及候选二维半导体建立不同磁序、应变、层间滑移和载流子掺杂的DFT+U加SOC数据集，拟合等变自旋哈密顿量而非只拟合标量能量。用磁性图网络预测交换、Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya和单离子各向异性，再以Wannier插值计算动量空间共振、Berry曲率和非线性输运。验证应包括独立磁序的DFT总能、非共线自洽结果、线性自旋波或自旋动力学，以及对称性允许的手性响应随应变和栅压变化。

  5. 05
    文献研究：Quantum Hall Ferromagnetism in a Cavity Vacuum
    Quantum Hall Ferromagnetism in a Cavity Vacuum
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：研究腔体真空涨落驱动量子霍尔铁磁体发生连续相变，并通过自旋波理论、平均场理论和 DMRG 分析紧致核心相。主要结论是，空间反对称的腔场在足够强的光物质耦合下会诱导非均匀电子密度和新的磁性结构。

    📐 方法要点：文章研究填充因子ν=1量子霍尔铁磁体在腔真空涨落作用下的连续相变。方法从偶极规范下的朗道能级投影出发，将电子与光子自由度表示为近似张量积态，再通过解析自旋模型或等效自由能分析腔场诱导的相互作用、序参量和临界行为。该问题的核心输入是朗道能级、光-物质耦合、腔频率、真空涨落强度及自旋交换参数；所给摘要没有给出具体截断、有限尺寸计算或临界指数，也未显示使用机器学习。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该工作与团队的磁性、量子功能材料和有效哈密顿量传统相连，但研究对象从晶格磁体转向量子霍尔电子体系，主导耦合是腔真空涨落而非通常的自旋-晶格或Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用。Hongjun Xiang可将其与磁电耦合、拓扑磁态和群论分析联系起来，Hongyu Yu可借鉴机器学习哈密顿量的降阶思想；Xin-Gao Gong和Ji-Hui Yang的输运方向则可承接腔调控后的能带和响应计算。它不是直接ML论文，价值在于提供可学习的低能模型标签。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可将腔耦合扩展到二维磁性材料或拓扑异质结，先用DFT、Wannier和线性响应计算交换参数、磁各向异性、轨道磁矩及光学矩阵元，再构造含腔模的有效哈密顿量。用等变模型学习不同层间堆垛、应变、外场和腔频率下的参数，输入蒙特卡洛或张量网络求相图。验证包括腔关闭与开启的能级重排、磁化率峰、有限尺寸标度、Berry响应及光谱函数，并检查模型降阶是否保留连续相变的位置和对称性。

  6. 06
    文献研究：Non-Abelian chiral spin liquid in a spin-1 antiferromagnet on the square lattice
    Non-Abelian chiral spin liquid in a spin-1 antiferromagnet on the square lattice
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：arXiv:2609.15061v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Non-Abelian chiral spin liquids (CSLs) host non-Abelian anyon excitations and are promising platforms for topological quantum computation. However, real…

    📐 方法要点：研究考察方格晶格自旋1反铁磁模型中的非阿贝尔手性自旋液体，模型包含最近邻和次近邻双线性交换J1、J2以及双二次相互作用，可能还包括破坏时间反演的手性项。核心任务是从相图中识别非阿贝尔任意子、拓扑简并和手性边界态，通常需要精确对角化、变分波函数、张量网络或有限尺寸谱分析；摘要没有说明采用的具体数值算法、系统尺寸、边界条件和参数扫描范围，也没有机器学习成分。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该模型与Hongjun Xiang关注的自旋哈密顿量、非共线磁性、Kitaev相互作用、拓扑磁态及蒙特卡洛研究直接呼应。与CrI3、CrSBr等二维范德华磁体的区别在于，这里自旋为1且双二次交换和拓扑量子纠缠是核心，真实材料参数映射仍需从DFT+U和SOC计算建立。Hongyu Yu可将其与自旋-晶格动力学连接，Xin-Gao Gong可研究温度和声子影响；当前工作本身更偏理论模型，不能将其结果直接当作材料预测。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可从候选二维磁体的非共线DFT+U能量拟合双线性、双二次、各向异性交换和DMI，构造含自旋1或有效高自旋自由度的材料哈密顿量。用等变自旋网络学习局域环境到交换张量的映射，再以张量网络、精确对角化和有限温度蒙特卡洛交叉验证相图。对CrI3、CrSBr及层间滑移结构，应分别计算拓扑序、手性序参量、纠缠谱、边界态和外场响应，并评估缺陷、应变和自旋-晶格耦合是否摧毁非阿贝尔相。

    阅读原文 ↗
  7. 07
    文献研究：Physics-informed neural network (PINN) to predict vibrational stability of inorganic semiconductors
    Physics-informed neural network (PINN) to predict vibrational stability of inorganic semiconductors
    🎯 核心关注📖 ScienceDirect

    💡 亮点：论文题目表明其使用物理信息神经网络预测无机半导体的振动稳定性。该方法可能将晶体结构、振动或声子相关物理约束融入机器学习模型，以筛选具有稳定晶格动力学的半导体材料。

    📐 方法要点：该论文使用物理信息神经网络预测无机半导体的振动稳定性，目标是用机器学习近似或约束与声子稳定性相关的物理量。可行输入包括元素组成、晶体结构、晶格参数、原子坐标和局域邻接，输出可为声子频率、动力学稳定性标签或力常数矩阵；PINN应在数据损失之外加入平移旋转不变性、力常数对称性、声学和规则、负频模态惩罚等物理残差。公开摘要没有交代网络架构、训练集、声子计算设置、损失权重和测试精度，具体结论需以全文为准。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该方向与团队的ML势、晶格动力学、电子-声子耦合和高通量材料筛选相接。相比直接用DFT声子谱作监督，PINN试图将声子物理写入训练约束，可能降低数据需求并改善外推；但若只预测二分类稳定性，模型无法替代软模波矢、频率和特征向量信息。Xin-Gao Gong和Ji-Hui Yang可把稳定性结果接入输运、电子-声子耦合和半导体筛选，Hongyu Yu与Hongjun Xiang可使用等变架构和有效哈密顿量增强结构表达。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可先以Materials Project或团队现有DFT数据建立氧化物、硫族化物、卤化物和铁电候选的结构-力常数库，重点加入BaTiO3、HfO2、NbOI2及其极性相、非极性相和应变结构。模型输出完整或低秩力常数矩阵，并以声学和规则、空间群约束、声子非负性构造联合损失；对每个预测结构再用DFPT或有限位移DFT复核Γ点及全布里渊区软模。进一步以MACE或NEP进行有限温度MD，检查PINN预测的静态稳定性是否对应真实热稳定性和极化相变行为。

    阅读原文 ↗
  8. 08
    文献研究：Machine Learning Guided CALPHAD Design of Ru-Stabilized BCC B2 Refractory Alloys
    Machine Learning Guided CALPHAD Design of Ru-Stabilized BCC B2 Refractory Alloys
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：该工作将CALPHAD平衡相图计算与随机森林引导的主动学习结合，用于筛选含钌铌基耐火合金的组成和高温BCC+B2相稳定区间。研究发现适量Ru可显著提高B2相稳定温度，而Al可能促进竞争性的sigma和A15相形成。

    📐 方法要点：文章将机器学习与CALPHAD结合，用于设计Ru稳定的BCC-B2难熔合金。研究对象是具有BCC基体和有序B2析出相的高温合金，重点考察Ru对RuHf、RuZr、RuTi等B2相高温稳定性的影响。方法路线应包括由实验或第一性原理获得的相平衡、形成焓、溶解度和热力学参数，利用机器学习建立成分到相稳定性或CALPHAD参数的代理模型，再进行多元成分扫描和相图计算；摘要未给出具体ML模型、特征、训练集规模、温度窗口及验证误差。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该工作与团队高通量材料设计、机器学习势和物理约束材料模拟的共同点在于用代理模型压缩昂贵计算，并以热力学约束完成候选筛选。它不同于铁电、磁性和缺陷问题，核心标签是多组分相稳定性与析出相平衡，而非局域能量、交换参数或电子态。Hongjun Xiang的高通量结构筛选、Hongyu Yu的可迁移ML势和Xin-Gao Gong的主动学习方法可直接迁移；Ji-Hui Yang的界面与输运经验则适合评估析出相对热导和电子性质的影响。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可把同一流程用于含Hf、Zr、Ti的氧化物或卤化物功能材料相图，先以DFT计算不同有序/无序结构、缺陷和应变下的形成能，再用不确定性感知模型选择CALPHAD实验数据或新DFT点。对于BaTiO3、HfO2及NbOI2，应同时标注极性相、非极性相、氧空位和温度依赖自由能，避免只按0 K能量排序。验证采用独立DFT、声子稳定性、有限温度MD和实验相图或XRD可观测量；对磁性体系还需加入CrI3、CrSBr的磁序自由能与自旋-晶格贡献。

  1. 01
    文献研究：Evaluating Predicted Densities, Hamiltonians, and Density Matrices as Periodic SCF Initializers
    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-15🎯 相关度 10

    📝 简单总结：论文构建了包含电荷密度、哈密顿量和密度矩阵的 43851 个晶体数据集，并比较不同机器学习电子态作为 DFT 自洽场初始值的效果。结果表明，预测电荷密度可使约 91% 的体系加速收敛，中位数减少 3 次自洽迭代并实现约 1.18 倍的自洽场循环加速。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与团队发展的机器学习 Kohn-Sham 哈密顿量、电子密度预测、图神经网络和第一性原理计算加速方向高度直接相关。其闭环 SCF 性能评估框架和密度、哈密顿量、密度矩阵的对比方法可直接借鉴。

    💡 进一步工作建议：可进一步研究等变图神经网络对自旋极化、非共线磁性和含缺陷体系 SCF 初值的预测能力。还可将该方法拓展到自旋轨道耦合、带电缺陷和有限温度分子动力学中的动态电子结构计算。

  3. 03
    文献研究：Unbiased first-principles construction of complete tensorial spin Hamiltonians
    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-15🎯 相关度 10

    💡 进一步工作建议：可进一步将该方法推广到磁性异质结、莫尔超晶格和磁电多铁体系，研究应变、电场或界面对高阶交换作用的调控。建议重点评估其与现有局域自旋描述符、机器学习自旋哈密顿量和自旋-晶格动力学方法的兼容性。

    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-15🎯 相关度 9

    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-15🎯 相关度 9

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与团队的机器学习势、等变神经网络、图神经网络、主动学习、半导体材料和分子动力学方向直接高度相关。其关于验证误差与长时间动力学稳定性不一致、以及通过不确定性采样提升势函数可靠性的结论，对缺陷、界面、纳米材料和热输运模拟具有直接借鉴价值。

    💡 进一步工作建议：可将类似基准测试扩展到含缺陷、带电态、表面重构或异质界面的半导体体系，并比较不同模型对能量、力、应力、声子和输运性质的影响。建议进一步结合第一性原理主动学习，建立面向缺陷形成、载流子俘获和非绝热动力学的专用机器学习势。

  6. 06
    文献研究：B-type Quadratic Planar Hall Effect as a Probe of Altermagnetic Order
    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-15🎯 相关度 9

    📝 简单总结：提出B型二次平面霍尔效应作为识别交替磁序的新输运探针，指出该响应源于外磁场、磁序与电子能带量子几何性质的耦合。对KV2Se2O和RuO2的分析表明，该效应在交替磁相中允许，而在反铁磁非磁性相中被对称性禁止，并预计在约1特斯拉磁场下可观测。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与团队的交替磁性、非共线磁性、对称性分析、量子输运和磁性拓扑性质方向高度直接相关。该工作可借鉴其利用响应函数区分相近磁结构的思路，并结合第一性原理、Wannier插值和自旋轨道耦合计算进行验证。

    💡 进一步工作建议：建议计算更多交替磁材料中的二次平面霍尔响应，并系统分析磁畴、应变、载流子浓度和自旋轨道耦合的影响。还可利用自旋哈密顿量或机器学习自旋模型研究有限温度磁序涨落对该输运信号的作用。

    💡 进一步工作建议：可进一步将CALPHAD筛选与第一性原理形成能、弹性性质、声子稳定性和磁性计算结合，建立多尺度合金设计流程。也可阅读其主动学习采样和物理判据设计，以改进面向相变、缺陷或磁性材料的高通量搜索。

    💡 进一步工作建议：可进一步将第一性原理计算得到的缺陷能级、载流子有效质量、电子-声子耦合和复合参数输入物理信息模型，建立跨尺度的TiO₂光阳极输运模型。也可研究厚度、界面缺陷和光照条件共同作用下的载流子寿命与非辐射复合。

    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-15🎯 相关度 2

    📝 简单总结：该论文使用基于Psiformer的神经网络变分蒙特卡洛方法预测LiH、OH、TiH和NiH等体系的总能量与过渡金属氢化物键解离能，并与高精度耦合簇计算及实验数据进行比较。研究重点是强电子相关、相对论效应和核量子效应对小分子键能预测的影响。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：论文与我们的机器学习电子结构和量子模拟方法存在较弱联系，但主要面向小分子量子化学和神经网络波函数，不直接涉及凝聚态材料、晶体结构、材料缺陷或功能性质。其变分蒙特卡洛和零方差外推思想对发展高精度电子结构模型具有方法学参考价值。

    💡 进一步工作建议：若团队关注机器学习波函数或强关联体系，可进一步考察其在周期性边界条件、固体材料和自旋极化体系中的可扩展性。否则可将其作为神经网络电子结构方法的补充背景阅读，而非优先跟进方向。

  46. 46
    磁性与非磁性胶体中的玻璃态特征与动力学阻滞
    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-15🎯 相关度 1

    📝 简单总结：论文使用 Stockmayer 模型和 Lennard-Jones 模型分子动力学，比较磁性与非磁性胶体在低温淬火后的结构和玻璃化动力学。结果表明，偶极相互作用会促进形成分支状开放结构，并导致非高斯位移分布和动态非均匀性。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：仅在分子动力学、磁偶极相互作用和玻璃化动力学方面与团队方法存在弱关联，但研究对象是软物质胶体，不属于团队重点关注的凝聚态功能材料和电子结构问题。

    💡 进一步工作建议：若关注方法迁移，可参考其动态异质性和非高斯位移分析，用于研究磁性材料中的自旋-晶格动力学或离子迁移。除此之外，优先级较低。

今日摘要

总览：今日共收录72篇文献，覆盖机器学习、计算物理和功能材料方向。

热点：文献主要涉及结构—性质建模、有限温度/动力学模拟以及材料电子与磁性问题；缺少原文细节的条目不作定量推断。

与我们研究方向的总体关系：本日文献与团队研究的交集主要集中在强化学习和闭环控制、主动学习和高通量采样、分子动力学与有限温度轨迹、密度泛函理论和电子结构计算，并落到晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系、缺陷、畴壁、成核和开关动力学、声子、有限温度和输运性质、带电缺陷、缺陷形成能、陷阱态和器件退化。对现有工作最直接的价值是提供可复用的表示、采样或验证流程，而不是替代具体材料体系的物理判断。后续筛选应优先核对原文数据是否包含结构、能量、力、应力、极化、自旋或有限温度轨迹，再决定是否迁移到铁电、磁性、缺陷和外场驱动模拟。

🔬 神经网络势 · 电子结构（重点）

  1. 01
    文献研究：Prescreening Point Defects in Semiconductors With Machine Learning#
    Prescreening Point Defects in Semiconductors With Machine Learning#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Paul Karlsson, Joel Davidsson, Rickard Armiento
    相关度
    9
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.14846v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: High-throughput calculations using density-functional theory (DFT) are commonly used to explore point defects for applications in power electronics and quantum technologies. There is currently a major shift away from these traditional simulation techniques towards machine learning (ML) methods. We explore a class of physics-guided ML models for predicting defect formation energies and zero-phonon lines (ZPL) to identify point defects for quantum applications. The models are specifically targeted for use in a prescreening step for accelerated high-throughput workflows, and are therefore designed to avoid the costly relaxation step typically present with ML interatomic potentials (MLIPs). We compare performance for single and double point defect systems in 4H-SiC with ridge, kernel ridge, and multilayer perceptron (MLP) models using three different descriptors representing the defect systems. For vacancies and substitutions, the optimized models give mean absolute errors (MAEs) of 0.437 eV for the formation energy and 0.202 eV for ZPLs, which is just above the level at which such predictions can be useful even beyond the targeted prescreening, i.e., in some applications they may completely replace the need for costly DFT calculations. For interstitials the MAEs are larger, 1.101 eV for the formation energy and 0.230 eV for the ZPL, which, while still useful for prescreening, will not generally be useful for more detailed characterization. Hence, while the results may be further improved by model design and optimization, the models presented in this work are already useful for prescreening in high-throughput characterization of point defects.

    💡 亮点：本文构建物理引导的机器学习模型，用于高通量预筛选半导体点缺陷的形成能和零声子线，目标是避免传统缺陷结构弛豫带来的计算开销。研究以4H-SiC中的空位和替位缺陷为例，比较岭回归、核岭回归和多层感知机模型，获得了具有实用潜力的预测精度。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：文章研究用物理引导机器学习对半导体点缺陷进行预筛选，目标是在高通量DFT之前预测缺陷相关性质。合理的路线是以宿主结构、缺陷元素、空位或替位类型、电荷态、局域配位和化学势条件构造特征，输出形成能、跃迁能级、带隙内位置或缺陷稳定性；模型应通过物理约束、对称性编码、能量参考和化学空间划分减少数据偏差。所给摘要没有提供具体模型、标签、数据集规模和物理损失形式，不能据此断言采用了某一特定GNN或数值精度。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该方向与团队在Si、SiC、GaN、SiO2、WSe2、CrI3和卤化物钙钛矿中的缺陷形成能、电荷修正、深能级及非辐射复合研究高度一致。它区别于直接训练缺陷性质回归器的地方，在于先做候选压缩，再把昂贵DFT集中于高价值构型；但若忽略电荷态、费米能级、化学势和有限尺寸修正，模型容易学习到错误相关性。Xin-Gao Gong、Ji-Hui Yang的缺陷与输运工作可提供标签体系，Hongyu Yu和Hongjun Xiang可贡献物理约束GNN及张量性质模型。

    💡 对你方向的启示：建议先整理SiC色心、GaN空位、WSe2硫属空位、CrI3带电缺陷和钙钛矿反位缺陷数据库，统一超胞尺寸、化学势窗口、DFT泛函、DFT+U参数和电荷修正协议。用分层交叉验证按化学元素和缺陷类型留出，避免同一宿主的相似构型泄漏；模型输出候选排序及不确定度，再对前若干百分比进行自洽DFT、GW或BSE校验。最终把形成能预筛选与非辐射复合矩阵元、缺陷扩散NEB和载流子寿命连接起来，验证筛选是否真正改善器件相关指标。

  2. 02
    文献研究：Bayesian neural networks versus deep ensembles for uncertainty quantification in machine learning interatomic potentials#
    Bayesian neural networks versus deep ensembles for uncertainty quantification in machine learning interatomic potentials#
    🧭 化学 / 分子P1 · AI×化学·材料📖 Machine Learning: Science and Technology👤 Riccardo Farris, Emanuele Telari, Nongnuch Artrith, Konstantin M Neyman, Albert Bruix
    相关度
    8
    📖 英文原文

    Neural-network-based machine learning interatomic potentials have emerged as powerful tools for predicting atomic energies and forces, enabling accurate and efficient simulations in atomistic modeling. A key limitation of traditional deep learning approaches, however, is their inability to provide reliable estimates of predictive uncertainty. Such uncertainty quantification is critical for assessing model reliability, especially in materials science, where often the model is applied on out-of-distribution data. Different strategies have been proposed to address this challenge, with deep ensembles (DE) and Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) being among the most widely used. In this work, we introduce an implementation of BNNs with variational inference in the ænet-PyTorch framework. To evaluate their applicability to machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs), we systematically compare the performance of variational BNNs (VBNNs) and DE on a dataset of 7815 TiO2 structures, and further assess the generalizability of our conclusions on the chemically more diverse QM7 dataset of 7165 small organic molecules. The models are trained on both a full dataset and a smaller subset to assess how variations in data representation influence predictive accuracy and uncertainty estimation. Our findings reveal that, despite its more empirical and less theory-grounded nature and in addition to its lower inference cost and implementation simplicity, DE outperform VBNNs on most accuracy and uncertainty-quantification metrics across data regimes, the main exceptions being the calibration error and the energy accuracy on the chemically diverse QM7 dataset, where the BNNs are competitive or better. This analysis provides insights into the strengths and limitations of each approach, offering practical guidance for the development and evaluation of uncertainty-aware MLIPs.

    💡 亮点：本文比较变分贝叶斯神经网络与深度集成方法在机器学习原子间势中的不确定性量化能力，并在TiO2结构和QM7分子数据集上评估其预测可靠性与泛化能力。研究重点是识别分布外结构、估计模型置信度，并提高机器学习势在原子模拟中的安全使用能力。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：文章比较贝叶斯神经网络与深度集成在机器学习原子间势不确定性量化中的表现。两类模型都以原子结构为输入、以总能和原子力为监督目标；BNN通过权重后验或变分近似传播参数不确定性，deep ensemble则训练多个不同初始化、数据扰动或小批次顺序的独立势函数，再由预测均值和方差估计认知不确定性。所给摘要未披露具体网络、数据规模、先验、集成数和校准指标，复现时必须记录这些设置，并分别检验能量、力及外推构型上的覆盖率。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该工作直接呼应团队发展的机器学习势、主动学习和等变图神经网络，核心差别是把力场精度评价推进到置信度可靠性，而不是只报告测试集MAE。BNN适合表达参数后验，但大模型变分推断成本高；deep ensemble易并行、工程稳定，却可能低估共同数据偏差。对Hongyu Yu和Hongjun Xiang的ML势及ML Kohn-Sham哈密顿量尤其相关，也连接Xin-Gao Gong在缺陷、非绝热动力学中的主动学习需求；Weibin Chu的长时间载流子动力学同样需要这种失效预警。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可在NbOI2、BaTiO3和CrSBr上建立DFT或DFT+U构型池，覆盖平衡态、极化翻转路径、畴壁、应变、自旋旋转和高温MD，再训练等变BNN与MACE deep ensemble。用能量/力误差、预测区间覆盖率、负对数似然和MD能量漂移联合筛选构型；对高不确定度结构回算DFT并迭代主动学习。最终以NEP或高精度MACE长时MD复核相变温度、畴壁迁移和自旋-晶格耦合，并比较不同不确定性指标对灾难性外推的提前预警能力。

    阅读原文 ↗

    文献研究：Evaluating Predicted Densities, Hamiltonians, and Density Matrices as Periodic SCF Initializers#
    Evaluating Predicted Densities, Hamiltonians, and Density Matrices as Periodic SCF Initializers#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Pin Chen, Jiang Li, Yutong Lu
    相关度
    10
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.15151v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Learned electronic states are usually evaluated by prediction error, even though their intended use is to accelerate the self-consistent-field (SCF) loop of density functional theory (DFT). We ask whether lower offline error actually yields a better SCF initializer. We construct ρHD-43K, a 43,851-crystal DFT corpus with aligned charge-density, Hamiltonian, and density-matrix labels, and extend the solver workflow to inject all three predicted states. The closed-loop benchmark compares direct converged-state prediction against residual prediction from solver-native references on a frozen test set of non-magnetic crystals, using paired convergence, iteration, and SCF-loop timing measurements. Injecting the exact converged density matrix or Hamiltonian as an oracle upper bound cuts the median SCF count from 16 to one, revealing substantial acceleration headroom. Among learned inputs, direct charge-density prediction (Charge3Net-E3) accelerates about 91% of paired crystals, saves a median of three SCF iterations, and yields a 1.18× SCF-loop speedup. In contrast, the tested residual-density and matrix initializers do not consistently improve over the standard superposition-of-atomic-densities baseline. These results establish that target-space accuracy alone is insufficient: a learned initializer must also be compatible with the nonlinear solver trajectory, and its utility must be measured in the loop.

    💡 亮点：论文构建了包含电荷密度、哈密顿量和密度矩阵的 43851 个晶体数据集，并比较不同机器学习电子态作为 DFT 自洽场初始值的效果。结果表明，预测电荷密度可使约 91% 的体系加速收敛，中位数减少 3 次自洽迭代并实现约 1.18 倍的自洽场循环加速。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：密度泛函理论和电子结构计算, 有效哈密顿量或机器学习哈密顿量，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”补充训练/验证集，再比较《Evaluating Predicted Densities, Hamiltonians, and Density Matrices as Periodic SCF Initializers》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    阅读原文 ↗

  4. 04
    文献研究：Benchmarking Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials for Dynamical Stability in Inorganic Semiconductor Nanocrystals: A CdSe Case Study#
    Benchmarking Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials for Dynamical Stability in Inorganic Semiconductor Nanocrystals: A CdSe Case Study#
    🧭 化学 / 分子P1 · AI×化学·材料📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 M. Usman, M. Suleymanova, Z. U. Abideen, M. Fernández-Pendás, I. Infante
    相关度
    9
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.15299v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) enable nanosecond-scale atomistic simulations of inorganic semiconductor nanocrystals, but low errors on held-out configurations do not necessarily guarantee stable molecular dynamics. We benchmark five graph-neural-network MLIPs, SchNet, PaiNN, NequIP, Allegro and MACE, for dynamical stability in a chloride-passivated cadmium selenide nanocluster containing 149 atoms. The models were trained under harmonized conditions on 1,000 configurations and evaluated against 2,000 held-out configurations generated using density functional theory, considering force accuracy, computational efficiency, uncertainty and structural stability during 1 ns simulations at 300 K. Allegro produced the lowest validation force mean absolute errors, ranging from 12 to 14 meV per angstrom, whereas SchNet produced the largest, ranging from 84 to 110 meV per angstrom. This ranking did not predict dynamical robustness: NequIP remained stable for 1 ns without additional training data, whereas MACE became stable only after an ensemble-based active-learning procedure added 34 uncertainty-selected configurations. PaiNN and Allegro remained unstable after the addition of 100 and 95 configurations, respectively, and SchNet also failed to achieve stable dynamics within the tested augmentation budget. Under the benchmark hardware conditions, the machine-learning potentials required 6-66 ms per molecular-dynamics step, compared with approximately 20 s for density functional theory. These results show that held-out prediction errors alone are insufficient for selecting interatomic potentials for finite, surface-dominated nanostructures. Reliable deployment requires long-timescale dynamical testing combined with uncertainty-guided refinement, while the effectiveness and data efficiency of active learning remain strongly architecture dependent.

    💡 亮点：论文系统比较SchNet、PaiNN、NequIP、Allegro和MACE五种图神经网络机器学习原子间势在氯钝化CdSe纳米晶中的力精度、效率、不确定性和长时间分子动力学稳定性。研究发现，静态验证误差排名不能可靠预测动力学稳定性，NequIP具有较好的初始稳定性，而MACE需要结合集成模型主动学习补充数据后才能实现稳定模拟。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：该研究以CdSe无机半导体纳米晶为案例，比较SchNet、PaiNN、NequIP、Allegro和MACE五种图神经网络原子间势，重点不再是留出集误差，而是分子动力学中的动力学稳定性。输入应包括原子种类、三维坐标和局域邻接关系，模型输出总能并通过坐标梯度得到力；标量SchNet、带方向信息的PaiNN以及E(3)等变NequIP、Allegro、MACE形成逐步增强的几何表达。摘要未给出截断半径、层数、数据划分、时间步长和温度范围，这些参数是判断结论可迁移性的关键。

    🔗 相关工作关联：研究回应了团队长期使用ML势进行大尺度分子动力学时的核心风险：低测试误差并不保证轨迹稳定、能量守恒和结构物理性。PaiNN、NequIP、Allegro、MACE与团队的等变GNN路线一致，而SchNet可作为不含显式等变方向基线；与传统NEP相比，图网络通常更易表达复杂局域环境，但训练和推理成本可能更高。该CdSe纳米晶问题也连接Ji-Hui Yang的二维/纳米半导体、Xin-Gao Gong的缺陷动力学以及Weibin Chu的长时间非绝热模拟。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可把同一基准迁移到含表面配体、Cd空位、Se空位和应变的CdSe或钙钛矿纳米晶，并扩展到CrI3、CrSBr薄片的热稳定性。训练集除随机扰动和DFT平衡构型外，应加入高温轨迹、表面重构、缺陷扩散和不同自旋构型；磁性体系需使用DFT+U并显式标注自旋。用短轨迹外推测试、长时NVE能量漂移、径向分布函数、配位数、键长分布和相变温度验证，比较MACE/Allegro与NEP在计算成本和稳定性上的取舍。

    阅读原文 ↗

    文献研究：A cylindrical sintering method for more realistic grain boundaries in nanocrystalline thin films#
    A cylindrical sintering method for more realistic grain boundaries in nanocrystalline thin films#
    🧭 化学 / 分子P1 · 其他📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Ankit Yadav, Lucia Bajtošová, Miroslav Cieslar, Jan Fikar
    相关度
    6
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.15246v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Discrepancies between simulated and experimental mechanical properties in molecular dynamics simulations of nanocrystalline metals typically arise from the sample-construction method and the interatomic potential choice. We introduce a cylindrical sintering method to generate nanocrystalline aluminum thin-film samples with wider, more disordered grain boundaries than the usual Voronoi tessellation method, while maintaining deterministic control over grain size, shape, and orientation. Cylindrical sintered samples are benchmarked against hexagonal Voronoi references under identical conditions using both the classical Pascuet15 MEAM and tabGAP machine-learning potentials. Cylindrical sintered samples consistently show lower mechanical properties than hexagonal Voronoi samples due to their wider, more disordered grain boundaries - an effect independent of the choice of potential. Notably, changing the sample geometry and changing the interatomic potential produce comparable, additive, and independent shifts in predicted properties, highlighting that future molecular dynamics studies must hold both variables fixed for meaningful comparisons. Common neighbor and dislocation extraction analyses confirm that deformation is dominated by grain-boundary-mediated plasticity. Uniaxial tensile tests reveal an inverse Hall-Petch relationship for both sample types and both potentials, with mechanical properties decreasing monotonically as grain size reduces from 40.34 to 4.84 nm. The cylindrical sintering method offers a physically realistic, geometrically controlled alternative that bridges idealized Voronoi models and disordered experimental grain-boundary structures.

    💡 亮点：提出圆柱烧结方法生成更接近真实的纳米晶铝薄膜晶界结构，并比较其与六边形Voronoi结构在分子动力学中的力学性质差异。研究发现晶界几何和机器学习势都会显著影响预测结果，且二者的影响近似独立并具有可加性。

    📐 方法要点：方法上，摘要目前只能确认：分子动力学与有限温度轨迹。需要回到原文核对数据来源、标签定义、模型架构、物理约束和验证集，不能把题名直接等同于可迁移算法。若要接入团队的 ML 势或 Hamiltonian 流程，应先确认它是否同时学习能量、力、应力、自旋、极化或电子结构，以及是否报告跨材料/跨温度外推。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    文献研究：Cracking the case: fluctuations enhance ductility in refractory alloys#
    Cracking the case: fluctuations enhance ductility in refractory alloys#
    🧭 化学 / 分子P1 · 其他📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Manura Liyanage, Julia Chmielewska, Tijmen Vermeij, Johann Michler, Christian Leinenbach, W. A. Curtin 等7位作者
    相关度
    6
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13465v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Refractory body-centered cubic (BCC) alloys are attractive candidates for structural applications at extreme temperatures, yet combining room-temperature ductility with high-temperature strength remains the unsolved challenge. Ductility in crystals requires that dislocations emit from a sharp crack tip before brittle cleavage, but continuum theories that treat disordered alloys as chemically homogeneous incorrectly predict brittleness for many experimentally ductile alloys. Here we show that atomic-scale stress fluctuations in disordered alloys create an additional local stress intensity at the crack tip, enabling dislocation loop nucleation below the cleavage threshold. Accounting for these fluctuations yields a local ductility criterion where alloys deemed brittle by conventional fracture mechanics can be intrinsically ductile. Atomistic simulations with machine-learned interatomic potentials and an analytic fracture mechanics model correctly predict composition-driven brittle-to-ductile transitions in binary and ternary Mo-Nb-Ti alloys, in Nb-Ti alloys at 4 K, and in several commercial BCC alloys at room temperature. Guided by this criterion, we predict, fabricate, and test the Hf₁₅Mo₁₅Nb₃₂Ti₃₈ alloy, confirming its room-temperature ductility. This fluctuation-driven mechanism provides a quantitative basis for designing ductile multicomponent BCC alloys.

    💡 亮点：该论文研究难熔体心立方合金中的脆性—延性转变，指出原子尺度应力涨落能够促进裂纹尖端位错环成核，从而提高材料延性。作者结合机器学习原子间势、原子尺度模拟和断裂力学模型，预测了Mo-Nb-Ti、Nb-Ti及部分商业合金的成分驱动延性变化。

    文献研究：Symmetry- and Property-Aware Crystal Generation with Reinforcement Learning for Inverse Materials Design#
    Symmetry- and Property-Aware Crystal Generation with Reinforcement Learning for Inverse Materials Design#
    🧭 化学 / 分子P3 · 铁电·极化📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Ting-Wei Hsu, Arun Bansil, Qimin Yan
    相关度
    8
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13468v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The inverse design of crystalline materials ultimately seeks structures with desired physical properties. However, for many functional responses, a favorable numerical value is meaningful only when supported by the symmetry of the underlying crystal. Without the appropriate crystallographic constraints, an apparent response may be ill defined, accidental, or not symmetry protected. Here we introduce SPARC, a symmetry- and property-aware reinforcement learning framework that optimizes physical objectives while preserving the structural conditions required for their realization. We demonstrate SPARC on two complementary tasks. The first targets strong uniaxial dielectric anisotropy, a tensorial response that is well defined only within appropriate crystal classes. The second maximizes the spectroscopic limited maximum efficiency, a scalar device-level objective without a prescribed symmetry class, allowing the framework to identify favorable crystallographic motifs. These results show that symmetry is not merely an additional design constraint, but a physical foundation for generating candidates with meaningful, robust, and realizable functional properties.

    💡 亮点：提出SPARC对称性与性质感知的强化学习晶体生成框架，在满足晶体对称性约束的同时优化目标物性。该方法分别用于设计强单轴介电各向异性材料和提高光谱限制最大效率，强调对称性是获得物理上可实现性质的基础。

    文献研究：Physics-informed neural network (PINN) to predict vibrational stability of inorganic semiconductors#
    Physics-informed neural network (PINN) to predict vibrational stability of inorganic semiconductors#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · AI×物理📖 ScienceDirect
    相关度
    6
    📖 英文原文

    Publication date: 5 September 2026 Source: Computational Materials Science, Volume 274 Author(s): M.H. Zeb, M.Z. Kabir

    💡 亮点：论文题目表明其使用物理信息神经网络预测无机半导体的振动稳定性。该方法可能将晶体结构、振动或声子相关物理约束融入机器学习模型，以筛选具有稳定晶格动力学的半导体材料。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：该论文使用物理信息神经网络预测无机半导体的振动稳定性，目标是用机器学习近似或约束与声子稳定性相关的物理量。可行输入包括元素组成、晶体结构、晶格参数、原子坐标和局域邻接，输出可为声子频率、动力学稳定性标签或力常数矩阵；PINN应在数据损失之外加入平移旋转不变性、力常数对称性、声学和规则、负频模态惩罚等物理残差。公开摘要没有交代网络架构、训练集、声子计算设置、损失权重和测试精度，具体结论需以全文为准。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该方向与团队的ML势、晶格动力学、电子-声子耦合和高通量材料筛选相接。相比直接用DFT声子谱作监督，PINN试图将声子物理写入训练约束，可能降低数据需求并改善外推；但若只预测二分类稳定性，模型无法替代软模波矢、频率和特征向量信息。Xin-Gao Gong和Ji-Hui Yang可把稳定性结果接入输运、电子-声子耦合和半导体筛选，Hongyu Yu与Hongjun Xiang可使用等变架构和有效哈密顿量增强结构表达。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可先以Materials Project或团队现有DFT数据建立氧化物、硫族化物、卤化物和铁电候选的结构-力常数库，重点加入BaTiO3、HfO2、NbOI2及其极性相、非极性相和应变结构。模型输出完整或低秩力常数矩阵，并以声学和规则、空间群约束、声子非负性构造联合损失；对每个预测结构再用DFPT或有限位移DFT复核Γ点及全布里渊区软模。进一步以MACE或NEP进行有限温度MD，检查PINN预测的静态稳定性是否对应真实热稳定性和极化相变行为。

    文献研究：Machine Learning Guided CALPHAD Design of Ru-Stabilized BCC B2 Refractory Alloys#
    Machine Learning Guided CALPHAD Design of Ru-Stabilized BCC B2 Refractory Alloys#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Nathan Peterson, Avik Mahata, Nick Beaver, Mohsen Kivy
    相关度
    5
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.14925v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Refractory alloys with a ductile body-centered-cubic (BCC) matrix strengthened by ordered B2 precipitates offer a high-temperature analogue to the gamma/gamma-prime architecture of Ni-based superalloys. Ruthenium is particularly attractive as a B2 stabilizer because RuHf, RuZr, and RuTi can retain ordered phases well above 1300 C. In this work, equilibrium CALPHAD calculations were coupled with random-forest-guided active learning to explore a ten-element Nb-based, Ru-bearing composition space containing Nb, Ta, Mo, V, Ru, Ti, Zr, Hf, Al, and Y at 1 at.% resolution. Across 500 CALPHAD-evaluated alloys, the calculations reproduced the principal trends reported for the Ru-B2 design space. RuHf and RuZr remained stable to the solidus, RuTi commonly exhibited a solutionizing window, and Al-containing alloys preferentially formed competing sigma and A15 phases. The upper bound of the BCC+B2 field increased from a median of approximately 1570 C at 5 at.% Ru to approximately 1980 C near 9-10 at.% Ru. Among the group-IV additions, Hf, Zr, and Ti produced progressively lower two-phase stability. Re-screening using physically motivated criteria identified 100 alloys satisfying requirements for high-temperature BCC+B2 stability, absence of liquid, phase purity, and appropriate secondary-phase fraction, including 19 Ru-lean compositions and two independently reported HfRu-B2 alloys. The results establish practical compositional design rules for Ru-stabilized dual-phase refractory alloys and identify phase-specific BCC/B2 lattice misfit as a key target for future design cycles.

    💡 亮点：该工作将CALPHAD平衡相图计算与随机森林引导的主动学习结合，用于筛选含钌铌基耐火合金的组成和高温BCC+B2相稳定区间。研究发现适量Ru可显著提高B2相稳定温度，而Al可能促进竞争性的sigma和A15相形成。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：文章将机器学习与CALPHAD结合，用于设计Ru稳定的BCC-B2难熔合金。研究对象是具有BCC基体和有序B2析出相的高温合金，重点考察Ru对RuHf、RuZr、RuTi等B2相高温稳定性的影响。方法路线应包括由实验或第一性原理获得的相平衡、形成焓、溶解度和热力学参数，利用机器学习建立成分到相稳定性或CALPHAD参数的代理模型，再进行多元成分扫描和相图计算；摘要未给出具体ML模型、特征、训练集规模、温度窗口及验证误差。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该工作与团队高通量材料设计、机器学习势和物理约束材料模拟的共同点在于用代理模型压缩昂贵计算，并以热力学约束完成候选筛选。它不同于铁电、磁性和缺陷问题，核心标签是多组分相稳定性与析出相平衡，而非局域能量、交换参数或电子态。Hongjun Xiang的高通量结构筛选、Hongyu Yu的可迁移ML势和Xin-Gao Gong的主动学习方法可直接迁移；Ji-Hui Yang的界面与输运经验则适合评估析出相对热导和电子性质的影响。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可把同一流程用于含Hf、Zr、Ti的氧化物或卤化物功能材料相图，先以DFT计算不同有序/无序结构、缺陷和应变下的形成能，再用不确定性感知模型选择CALPHAD实验数据或新DFT点。对于BaTiO3、HfO2及NbOI2，应同时标注极性相、非极性相、氧空位和温度依赖自由能，避免只按0 K能量排序。验证采用独立DFT、声子稳定性、有限温度MD和实验相图或XRD可观测量；对磁性体系还需加入CrI3、CrSBr的磁序自由能与自旋-晶格贡献。

    文献研究：Physics-Informed Neural Modeling of TiO 2 Photoanode Thickness-Dependent Electron Transport and Photovoltaic Performance in Dye-Sensitized Solar Cells#
    Physics-Informed Neural Modeling of TiO 2 Photoanode Thickness-Dependent Electron Transport and Photovoltaic Performance in Dye-Sensitized Solar Cells#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · AI×物理📖 Preprints👤 K Sanyasi Naidu, Nalluri Mohan Rao
    相关度
    5
    📖 英文原文

    The thickness of a photoanode in dye-sensitized solar cells (DSSCs) simultaneously influences the dye-accessible volume, optical path length, electron-transport distance and recombination. To estimate this competition, a physics-informed neural network was constructed based on the six compressed TiO2 photoanodes used by Tsai et al. The spatial state is expressed as a random-feature (RF) neural field uθ(xi, d), with normalized depth, xi = x/d, and film thickness, d. The field is bound by a steady-state diffusion-generation-recombination equation, and extraction and zero-flux boundary conditions. In the 6 devices dataset, the contact-gradient current was mapped with a low-capacity regularized thickness correction, using the PDE result as a fixed value (inverse parameters effective electron diffusion coefficient, electron lifetime, effective photon flux and photon flux). The analytic solution of contact current and relative least square (L2) error of 0.997–1.001 and 0.08–0.68% respectively confirmed the neural field. The last device model resulted in training Mean Absolute Percentage Errors (MAPEs) of 7.03% for JSC, 1.00% for VOC, 1.55% for FF, and 6.44% for algebraically consistent efficiency. The efficiency MAPE of leave-one-thickness-out validation outperformed the results of linear regression (27.76%), quadratic regression (26.08%), natural cubic spline interpolation (78.42%), Gaussian-process regression (19.34%), and a conventional ANN (20.15%). The continuous model gave optimum thickness as 29.72 µm at 8.70% efficiency, while jackknife resampling revealed that the thickness has a significant uncertainty, as only six devices exist. A frozen model transfer to an independent published thickness series yielded the MAPE of 17.10%, indicating a low level of cross-system transfer, and a need for morphology and electrolyte-aware inputs. The study thus presents a physics-regularized small data framework that discriminates between within-system predictive value and universal DSSC generalization, which can be replicated.

    💡 亮点：论文针对染料敏化太阳能电池中TiO₂光阳极厚度对电子输运、复合和光伏性能的影响，构建了受扩散-产生-复合方程和边界条件约束的物理信息神经网络。模型能够较准确地拟合不同厚度器件的短路电流、电压和填充因子等性能，并量化厚度变化带来的输运与复合竞争。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入非绝热分子动力学、电子—声子耦合、Wannier/GW-BSE 和载流子输运。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及声子、有限温度和输运性质对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“先用高精度电子结构和声子/电子—声子矩阵元建立小规模基准，再训练可迁移 Hamiltonian 或 ML 势，最后在长时间尺度非绝热 MD、输运或界面电荷转移中验证”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：非绝热分子动力学、电子—声子耦合、Wannier/GW-BSE 和载流子输运已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到卤化物钙钛矿、二维半导体、FeSe、光电界面和能源材料。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注声子、有限温度和输运性质在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    文献研究：Adaptive neural quantum states: a recurrent neural network perspective#
    Adaptive neural quantum states: a recurrent neural network perspective#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · AI×物理📖 Machine Learning: Science and Technology👤 Jake McNaughton, Mohamed Hibat-Allah
    相关度
    2
    📖 英文原文

    Neural-network quantum states (NQS) are powerful neural-network ansätzes that have emerged as promising tools for studying quantum many-body physics through the lens of the variational principle. These architectures are known to be systematically improvable by increasing the number of parameters. Here we demonstrate an Adaptive scheme to optimize NQSs, through the example of recurrent neural networks (RNNs), using a fraction of the computation cost while reducing training fluctuations and improving the quality of variational calculations targeting ground states of prototypical models in one- and two-spatial dimensions. This Adaptive framework reduces the computational cost through training small RNNs and reusing them to initialize larger RNNs. This work opens up the possibility for optimizing graphical processing unit resources deployed in large-scale NQS simulations.

    💡 亮点：提出了一种自适应神经网络量子态方法，通过逐步扩展循环神经网络并复用已有模型参数，降低量子多体基态变分计算的成本和训练波动。该方法在一维和二维典型模型中提高了计算效率与变分结果质量。

    利用图神经网络自动为CO₂同位素体分配AFGL量子数#
    Automated AFGL quantum number assignment for CO₂ isotopologues using a graph neural network#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Marco G. Barnfield, Sergei N. Yurchenko, Jonathan Tennyson
    相关度
    0

    📄 摘要：准确分配计算得到的分子能级量子数，是为辐射传输应用生成完整谱线表及线展宽参数时的一个关键瓶颈。本文提出了一套自动化流程，用于为12种稳定同位素体中、低于15000 cm⁻¹的CO₂计算转动—振动态分配美国空军地球物理实验室（AFGL）量子数。作者在经验能级（MARVEL）数据上以传导方式训练GraphSAGE图神经网络，利用同位素体间的微扰链以及同位素体内的转动阶梯边，将分配信息传播到未标注的计算态。物理唯一性由匈牙利算法求解器在具有相同多聚体、转动量子数和宇称的状态组内进行局部约束。一个包含五代的自举循环会将高置信度预测逐步提升为训练集，从而无需额外标注工作即可扩大覆盖范围。该流程为12种同位素体分配了224650个此前未标注的能级，占所有可用状态（包括由MARVEL得到的能级）的10.7%；在5000 cm⁻¹以下，覆盖率现已提高到97.4%。该流程还包含一种新的决策树方法，用于在非对称同位素体中将AFGL符号转换为Herzberg符号；其网络架构和匈牙利算法唯一性约束并不局限于CO₂。任何具有守恒的类多聚体量子数以及大量未标注计算态的分子系统，都自然适合作为该方法的目标，这为下一代大规模计算谱线表的自动量子数标注提供了路径。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13803v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Accurate quantum number assignment for calculated molecular energy levels is a critical bottleneck in generating line broadening parameters for comprehensive line lists for radiative transfer applications. We present an automated pipeline for assigning Air Force Geophysics Laboratory (AFGL) quantum numbers to CO2 calculated rovibrational states lying below 15,000cm⁻¹ across all 12 stable isotopologues. A GraphSAGE graph neural network is trained transductively on empirical (MARVEL) energy levels, exploiting inter-isotopologue perturbation chains and intra-isotopologue rotational ladder edges to propagate assignment information to unlabelled calculated states. Physical uniqueness is enforced locally by a Hungarian algorithm solver operating within groups of states sharing the same polyad, rotational quantum number, and parity. A five-generation bootstrap loop iteratively promotes high-confidence predictions into the training set, expanding coverage without additional labelling effort. The pipeline assigns 224,650 previously unlabelled states over 12 isotopologues, accounting for 10.7% of all available states (including MARVEL-derived levels), with coverage now increased to 97.4% below 5000cm⁻¹. The pipeline includes a novel decision tree method for converting AFGL to Herzberg notation in asymmetric isotopologues, while the architecture and Hungarian uniqueness enforcement are applicable beyond CO2, any molecular system with a conserved polyad-like quantum number and a large body of unlabelled computed states is a natural target for this approach, suggesting a pathway toward automated quantum number annotation for the next generation of large-scale computed line lists.

    💡 亮点：准确分配计算分子能级的量子数，是生成用于辐射传输应用的完整谱线表及其线展宽参数的关键瓶颈。本文建立了自动化流程，利用GraphSAGE图神经网络、同位素体间微扰链和同位素体内转动阶梯边，为12种稳定CO₂同位素体中低于15000 cm⁻¹的转振能级分配AFGL量子数。该流程还结合匈牙利算法进行物理唯一性约束，并通过自举训练扩大覆盖范围，成功为大量未标注能级完成分配，同时提出了非对称同位素体中AFGL符号向Herzberg符号转换的方法。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入机器学习 Hamiltonian、带电缺陷形成能、缺陷扩散和非辐射复合。从摘要能确认的线索包括：图神经网络结构—性质建模，以及AI for science 与材料/物理问题的结构化分析对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“把电荷态、有限尺寸修正、局域配位和缺陷迁移路径纳入标注；用小超胞 DFT+U/GW 或缺陷形成能基准校准，再扩展到大超胞和有限温度扩散”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    文献研究：Multimodal deep learning from spectra for small-molecule structure identification: enhancing robustness with mixed-condition training#
    Multimodal deep learning from spectra for small-molecule structure identification: enhancing robustness with mixed-condition training#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · AI×化学·材料📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Bowen Gao, Lei Zhu, Yiying Wang, Wenjie Yu
    相关度
    7.5
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.14360v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: In practical molecular characterization, small-molecule structure identification benefits from complementary spectroscopic evidence, but missing, degraded, or mismatched spectra challenge multimodal models. Herein, we incorporate domain knowledge from spectroscopy and chemistry into mixed-condition training for candidate structure reranking, using a reproducible evaluation protocol and mixture-of-experts (MoE) fusion. The protocol incorporates perturbations tailored to each spectroscopic modality and chemically informed spectrum replacements to cover variations in spectral availability, quality, and consistency. A total of 79,462 test samples were evaluated across 30 predefined conditions using simulated spectra from the Multimodal Spectroscopic Dataset (MSSD) for mass spectrometry (MS), infrared (IR) spectroscopy, and 1H and 13C nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR), with up to 128 hard candidate structures per sample. A controlled two-by-two factorial comparison of complete-input training versus mixed-condition training and vanilla concatenation versus MoE fusion, with matched evaluation conditions, showed that mixed-condition training provided the main gains in both architectures. For MoE, mean reciprocal rank (MRR), averaged equally across conditions, increased from 0.9203 to 0.9763, a relative increase of 6.08%. Recall at rank 1 (R@1), averaged over the same conditions, increased from 89.50% to 96.36%, an increase of 6.86 percentage points and a relative increase of 7.67%. IR-only and MS/MS-only MRR increased from 0.4337 to 0.9307 and from 0.3711 to 0.8575, reaching 2.15 and 2.31 times their respective baseline values, while complete-input performance remained high. These results support integrating domain knowledge into training-condition design to improve robustness, with further gains from MoE under mixed-condition training.

    💡 亮点：arXiv:2609.14360v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: In practical molecular characterization, small-molecule structure identification benefits from complementary spectroscopic evidence, but missing, degrad…

    文献研究：Operational Range Bounding in Spectroscopy: A Safety Cage Framework for Machine Learning Models#
    Operational Range Bounding in Spectroscopy: A Safety Cage Framework for Machine Learning Models#
    🧭 化学 / 分子P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Nikki Grens, Luís F. Simões, Kai Hou Yip, Theresa Lueftinger
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13514v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of black-box machine learning models in safety-critical space missions remains a significant challenge, particularly when ground-truth is unavailable for validation. Although machine learning models offer a powerful means to augment standard pipelines by extracting transmission spectra from complex exoplanetary light curves, their susceptibility to unmodelled instrument anomalies, stellar activity, and domain shifts introduces unquantified risks. This study evaluates a modular safety cage architecture that operates as a parallel monitoring layer to assess the validity of a prediction without modifying the underlying estimator. By monitoring different runtime indicators, including uncertainty quantification, out-of-domain detection, and influence functions, the framework constrains the model's operational domain to a verified region. A controlled evaluation is conducted under both in-domain and cross-domain conditions, using datasets from the 2019 and 2021 editions of the Ariel Data Challenges. The results reveal that model failure is multifaceted and that no single indicator captures all failure modes, demonstrating the need for indicator fusion. The application of safety-driven rejection strategies shows that a modest 20% reduction in data coverage results in error reductions between 45% and 65% across different domains and evaluation metrics. Using a formalised coverage-risk framework, a systematic analysis of indicator combinations is performed to identify configurations that maximise risk-ranking accuracy and optimise the trade-off between data coverage and scientific performance. Safety cages provide a transparent mechanism for detecting unreliable predictions and represent a critical step towards the safe deployment of data-driven models in scientific applications, such as astrophysics, where ground truth is seldom available.

    面向机器学习辅助光谱偏振测量的非对称等离激元纳米结构偶极取向提取#
    Extracting dipole orientations from asymmetric plasmonic nanostructures towards machine-learning-assisted spectropolarimetry#
    🧭 化学 / 分子P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 P. Christian Simo, Michaela Zbytovska, Annika Mildner, Lukas Lang, Melanie Sommer, Dieter P. Kern 等7位作者
    相关度
    5

    📄 摘要：在本研究中，研究人员利用不同线性偏振片分析角下的偏振暗场光谱，对具有各种非对称性的纳米颗粒进行方位取向分析。采用解析偶极模型对其进行建模后，该方法能够揭示其以电偶极远场强度表示的光谱行为。通过同时拟合一组分析器角度下的光谱，可以提取相应的偶极取向。在统计方法中，研究人员进一步利用机器学习算法研究所有不重复的排列组合。所得偶极取向的方位分布与由模拟和电子显微镜图像得到的几何取向高度吻合。直方图梯度提升回归器评估了测量设置对同时拟合数据集的影响，并将分析器角度的权重与等离激元系统中的非对称性联系起来。这种综合光谱学方法提高了偶极取向测量的准确性，并使现代机器学习模型能够解析纳米结构中可能存在的复杂特征。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.15661v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: In this work, nanoparticles with various asymmetries are analyzed for their azimuthal orientations using polarimetric dark-field spectroscopy at different analyzing angles of a linear polarizer. This approach reveals their spectral behavior in terms of electric far-field dipole intensities when modeled with an analytical dipole model. By simultaneously fitting the spectra from a set of analyzer angles, the respective dipole orientations are extracted. In a statistical approach, all non-repeating permutations are further studied with a machine learning algorithm. The resulting azimuthal distribution of dipole orientations coincides well with the geometric orientations derived from simulations and electron microscope images. A histogram gradient boosting regressor evaluates the impact of the measurement setup on the simultaneously fitted sets, linking the weights of the analyzer angles to the asymmetry in the plasmonic systems. This comprehensive spectroscopic method improves the accuracy of dipole orientation measurements and enables modern machine learning models to interpret potentially complex features of nanostructures.

    💡 亮点：本文利用不同线性偏振片分析角下的偏振暗场光谱，对具有不同非对称性的纳米颗粒进行方位取向分析，并通过解析偶极模型拟合多组光谱以提取偶极取向。统计研究结合机器学习算法，所得偶极取向的方位分布与由模拟和电子显微镜图像得到的几何取向高度一致。研究还利用直方图梯度提升回归器评估测量设置对拟合结果的影响，从而提高偶极取向测量的准确性，并帮助机器学习模型解析纳米结构中的复杂特征。

    文献研究：Large spin splitting metallic altermagnets from machine-learned design rules#
    Large spin splitting metallic altermagnets from machine-learned design rules#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Ali Sufyan, Brahim Marfoua, J. Andreas Larsson, Rickard Armiento, Erik van Loon
    相关度
    10
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.14051v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Altermagnets combine compensated magnetic order with momentum-dependent spin splitting, providing spin-polarized electronic states without a net magnetization. Metallic d-wave altermagnets are particularly promising because they can support time-reversal-odd spin currents in linear response, yet experimentally validated bulk realizations remain scarce. Here, we use high-throughput density-functional-theory data to relate the magnitude of altermagnetic band splitting to compositional, structural, and DFT-derived magnetic descriptors. An interpretable gradient-boosted model identifies two candidate-prioritization criteria: compact unit cells and magnetic sublattices that sustain sizable local moments. Guided by these trends, we screen tetragonal A₂XY Heusler compounds in space group P4/mmm. Among 307 structures, symmetry identifies 169 altermagnetic arrangements, of which 157 remain metallic altermagnets in DFT. Sixteen realize an altermagnetic collinear ground state, of which 15 are dynamically and mechanically stable and 10 also lie on or below the calculated thermodynamic hull. Six candidates exceed the CrSb splitting obtained under the same computational protocol, led by Co₂AlSc (Δ_max=2.24~eV) and Fe₂AlGe (2.07~eV). A Julliere-model estimate gives a tunneling magnetoresistance of up to 203% at the Fermi level for Co₂AlSc. These results identify a chemically tunable family of metallic d-wave altermagnets and demonstrate how interpretable machine learning can guide targeted first-principles searches.

    💡 亮点：论文利用高通量第一性原理数据和可解释机器学习模型，提取大自旋劈裂交替磁体的设计规则，并筛选四方Heusler化合物。研究发现紧凑晶胞和具有较大局域磁矩的磁性子晶格有利于获得较强自旋劈裂，并获得多个兼具金属性、稳定性和热力学可行性的候选材料。

    Multi4D：用于复杂材料界面结构确定的端到端神经网络#
    Multi4D: an end-to-end neural network for structural determination at complex material interfaces#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Haoran Zhang, Zian Mao, Shufen Chu, Xiaoya He, Yuyan Guan, Antong Yang 等9位作者
    相关度
    4

    📄 摘要：异质界面决定着功能材料的性能和退化过程，因此，将局域结构变化与宏观失效机制联系起来，对于指导未来材料设计至关重要。然而，结构异质性、物相重叠和局域无序会产生高度复杂的衍射特征，使得在大视场范围内以原子分辨率解释延展的过渡区域变得困难。本文提出Multi4D，这是一种基于物理信息神经网络的框架，用于利用四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D-STEM）自动进行多组分晶体学识别。该方法将潜空间扩散变换器用于受物理约束的风格转换，并结合旋转不变卷积神经网络进行与取向无关的分类，从而将多组分衍射数据集转换为确定性的晶体学图谱，准确率达到98.82%。此外，本文引入了“衍射推断结构复杂度”这一信息论熵指标，该指标由分类器的预测不确定性导出，用于量化局域结构歧义。我们将Multi4D应用于复杂超导异质结构、腐蚀合金表面以及退化固态电池界面，生成了具有高保真度的结构图谱，空间分辨率达到单纳米尺度。该框架为自动化显微分析建立了具有统计稳健性的分析范式，有助于工业质量控制以及界面设计原理的数据驱动发现。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.14348v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Heterogeneous interfaces dictate the performance and degradation of functional materials, making it essential to link local structural variations with macroscopic failure mechanisms to guide future materials design. Yet structural heterogeneity, phase overlap, and local disorder produce highly convoluted diffraction signatures, making extended transition regions difficult to interpret at atomic resolution across large fields of view. Here, we introduce Multi4D, a physics-informed neural network framework for automated multi-component crystallographic identification using four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM). By combining a latent-space Diffusion Transformer for physics-constrained style translation with a rotation-invariant convolutional neural network for orientation-agnostic classification, this approach translates multi-components diffraction datasets into deterministic crystallographic maps with 98.82% accuracy. In addition, we introduce Diffraction-Inferred Structural Complexity as an information-theoretic entropy metric derived from classifier predictive uncertainty that quantifies local structural ambiguity. We apply Multi4D to generate high-fidelity structural maps of complex superconducting heterostructures, corroded alloy surfaces, and degraded solid-state battery interfaces down to single-nanometer spatial resolution. This framework establishes a statistically robust analytical paradigm for automated microscopy, facilitating both industrial quality control and the data-driven discovery of interfacial design principles.

    💡 亮点：异质界面决定着功能材料的性能与退化过程，但结构异质性、物相重叠和局域无序会使衍射信号高度复杂。本文提出物理信息神经网络框架Multi4D，结合物理约束的扩散变换器和旋转不变卷积神经网络，实现基于4D-STEM的自动多组分晶体学识别。该方法能够以98.82%的准确率生成确定性的晶体学图谱，并通过分类器预测不确定性构建衍射推断结构复杂度指标，应用于多类复杂界面，实现单纳米级结构解析。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“在 DFT 能量/力/应力之外加入跨温度构型、相对自由能、熵或热膨胀信息；用主动学习补采相变、畴壁和相竞争区域，再用热力学积分、MC 或有限温度 MD 验证”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到HfO₂ 基铁电、二维滑移铁电、钙钛矿和磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在HfO₂ 基铁电、二维滑移铁电、钙钛矿和磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“在 DFT 能量/力/应力之外加入跨温度构型、相对自由能、熵或热膨胀信息；用主动学习补采相变、畴壁和相竞争区域，再用热力学积分、MC 或有限温度 MD 验证”补充训练/验证集，再比较《Multi4D：用于复杂材料界面结构确定的端到端神经网络》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    文献研究：Disentangling Operating Conditions from Intrinsic Adsorbent Properties in CO₂ Capture: An Explainable Machine Learning Study Across Four Adsorbent Categories#
    Disentangling Operating Conditions from Intrinsic Adsorbent Properties in CO₂ Capture: An Explainable Machine Learning Study Across Four Adsorbent Categories#
    🧭 化学 / 分子P3 · AI×化学·材料📖 Preprints👤 Rahma H. Thabet
    相关度
    3
    📖 英文原文

    CO₂ adsorption performance depends jointly on intrinsic material properties and experimental operating conditions, complicating adsorbent comparisons across the literature. Here, a curated database of 398 CO₂ adsorption measurements spanning four adsorbent categories, carbon-based, organic porous, inorganic porous, and metal oxide adsorbents, was analyzed using exploratory data analysis and explainable machine learning to identify the key descriptors controlling adsorption capacity. BET surface area, pore volume, temperature, CO₂ partial pressure, and adsorbent family were identified as the most informative predictors. An optimized Gradient Boosting model achieved the highest predictive accuracy (R² = 0.930, RMSE = 3.567 mmol g⁻¹, MAE = 1.496 mmol g⁻¹), while a Random Forest model was used for the subsequent SHAP-based explainability analysis. CO₂ partial pressure dominated predictions across the complete dataset, but its influence receded once pressure effects were minimized, revealing more balanced contributions from BET surface area, pore volume, temperature, and adsorbent family. The central contribution of this work is the quantitative demonstration that literature-derived adsorbent databases are confounded by an unequal distribution of testing pressures across material families, such that family-level performance rankings computed without accounting for this confound can misrepresent intrinsic material behaviour. More consistent reporting of micropore characteristics is recommended to improve future data-driven prediction of CO₂ adsorption.

    💡 亮点：该工作基于398组二氧化碳吸附实验数据，使用梯度提升、随机森林和SHAP分析区分吸附剂本征性质与实验操作条件的影响。结果表明，二氧化碳分压对预测最重要，而比表面积、孔体积、温度和吸附剂类别在控制压力影响后具有更平衡的贡献。

    📐 方法要点：方法上，摘要目前只能确认：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法。需要回到原文核对数据来源、标签定义、模型架构、物理约束和验证集，不能把题名直接等同于可迁移算法。若要接入团队的 ML 势或 Hamiltonian 流程，应先确认它是否同时学习能量、力、应力、自旋、极化或电子结构，以及是否报告跨材料/跨温度外推。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    文献研究：Nonlinear dynamics of random neural networks with second-order synaptic motifs#
    Nonlinear dynamics of random neural networks with second-order synaptic motifs#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Jun Yang, Hannah Choi
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.14251v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Classical theories of random neural networks typically assume independent connectivity, overlooking the local motif structures prevalent in biological circuits. Here, we investigate how four second-order synaptic motifs (chain, reciprocal, convergent, and divergent) shape the dynamics of nonlinear firing-rate networks. While previous studies have established that chain correlations generate outlier eigenvalues, we demonstrate that these motifs also jointly reshape the Jacobian eigenvalue bulk. Using the path-integral formalism, we derive a dynamic mean-field theory which reveals that the chain motif acts as a retarded feedback of the ensemble-mean activity through the response kernel, producing a rich repertoire of dynamical regimes, including ferromagnetic states and limit cycles. At sufficiently large magnitude, negative chain correlations produce a glassy, multistable regime that was previously mainly associated with partially symmetric networks. Our theory also distinguishes convergent from divergent motifs: divergent correlations primarily rescale temporal noise, while convergent correlations suppress temporal chaos by converting nonzero mean activity into quenched heterogeneity. Finally, analyses of the Lyapunov spectrum and participation-ratio dimension show that motif structure changes the geometry of chaotic activity, reducing entropy production and attractor dimensionality even when the effective spectral edge is held fixed. Together, these findings establish second-order motifs as a fundamental structural mechanism governing the dynamical regimes of local cortical circuits.

    文献研究：PiMiX 2.02: Toward AI-Driven Data Fusion in Radiographic Imaging and Tomography#
    PiMiX 2.02: Toward AI-Driven Data Fusion in Radiographic Imaging and Tomography#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Zhehui Wang, Shanny Lin, Nicholas Amano, Ramya Gurunathan, Katie Liu, Nathan E. Peterson 等10位作者
    相关度
    7.5
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13347v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: PiMiX (Physics-informed Meta-instrument for eXperiments) was introduced for multi-instrument, multi-experiment, and simulation-experiment data fusion in radiographic imaging and tomography (RadIT). Here we present PiMiX 2.02 as an evolving AI-enhanced cyber-physical meta-instrument integrating imaging sensors, near-sensor computing, data fusion, physics-informed inference, and human-supervised AI workflows across X-ray, neutron, and other modalities. Demonstrated capabilities include multimodal CMOS radiation imaging, simulation-assisted sub-pixel neutron localization, and edge-deployed optical-neural-network (ONN) inference; GPU and ONN implementations achieved greater than 96% precision for neutron-event detection with sub-micron localization. A further advance is human-in-the-loop agentic-AI co-analysis of X-ray and neutron images from inertial-confinement-fusion experiments. Beyond conventional preprocessing, the workflow generates competing feature hypotheses, ranks contours using physics-informed evidence, estimates confidence, and presents alternatives for human review. The same architecture adapts to different physics: X-ray analysis emphasizes dark, nonuniform ring structures using deformable closed paths and multi-scale evidence, whereas neutron analysis targets bright emission envelopes using fractional-emission levels, intensity gradients, and cross-filter persistence. We also highlight automated comparison of an as-designed stereolithography model with an X-ray CT reconstruction of an additively manufactured metal lattice. Together, these examples show progression from AI assistance in specific processing tasks to multi-task, multi-domain scientific co-analysis. PiMiX 2.0 further provides a pathway toward PRISM, a RadIT scientific foundation model, and tighter integration of diagnostics, digital representations, inference, and experimental control.

    广义标准材料框架内的数据驱动内聚区建模#
    Data-Driven Cohesive Zone Modeling within the Generalized Standard Materials Framework#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · AI×化学·材料📖 arXiv / physics👤 Sida Hao, Jinkyo Han, Bahador Bahmani
    相关度
    0

    📄 摘要：内聚区模型被广泛用于描述断裂和界面失效，但大多数模型都规定了针对具体问题的解析牵引—分离定律，并结合现象学的卸载和再加载规则，从而需要针对不同内聚行为建立专门模型。本文在广义标准材料框架内发展了一种统一的可学习内聚模型，其中材料响应由学习得到的本构函数生成，而基础热力学结构保持不变。表面自由能被分解为主动贡献和接触贡献，不可逆损伤演化由学习得到的依赖变形模式的损伤抗力控制。主动能量由输入凸神经网络表示，损伤抗力的倒数则由单调神经网络表示。凸性、单调性、归一化和损伤不可逆性被直接纳入本构表示之中。对损伤抗力倒数进行直接参数化可以给出显式损伤更新，并避免在本构评估过程中进行局部非线性求逆。材料点研究表明，该模型能够表示定性上不同的内聚响应，包括平台、延伸的软化尾部、不规则软化、非线性卸载、不同的I型和II型行为，以及若干经典的混合模式内聚定律。因此，该框架用一种具有热力学结构的统一表示，取代了依赖具体定律的模型构造，并能够从数据中学习具有广泛函数复杂度的内聚响应。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13629v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Cohesive zone models are widely used to describe fracture and interfacial failure, yet most formulations prescribe problem-specific analytical traction-separation laws together with phenomenological rules for unloading and reloading, leading to specialized models for different cohesive behaviors. This work develops a unified learnable cohesive formulation within the generalized standard materials framework, in which the response is generated from learned constitutive functions while the underlying thermodynamic structure remains fixed. The surface free energy is decomposed into active and contact contributions, and irreversible damage evolution is governed by a learned mode-dependent damage resistance. The active energy is represented by an input-convex neural network, while the inverse damage resistance is represented by a monotone neural network. Convexity, monotonicity, normalization, and damage irreversibility are incorporated directly into the constitutive representation. Direct parameterization of the inverse resistance yields an explicit damage update and avoids local nonlinear inversion during constitutive evaluation. Material-point studies show that the formulation can represent qualitatively distinct cohesive responses, including plateaus, extended softening tails, irregular softening, nonlinear unloading, distinct Mode I and Mode II behaviors, and several classical mixed-mode cohesive laws. The framework therefore replaces law-specific model construction with a single thermodynamically structured representation capable of learning cohesive responses of broad functional complexity from data.

    💡 亮点：内聚区模型广泛用于描述断裂和界面失效，但传统模型通常依赖针对具体问题设定的解析牵引—分离关系以及现象学卸载和再加载规则。本文在广义标准材料框架内提出统一的可学习内聚模型，在保持热力学结构固定的同时学习本构函数。通过输入凸神经网络表示主动表面自由能、用单调神经网络表示损伤抗力的倒数，并将凸性、单调性和损伤不可逆性直接纳入本构表示，该方法能够描述多种复杂的内聚响应。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开。从摘要能确认的线索包括：热力学自由能、相稳定与有限温度建模, 自由能面和热力学积分/采样，以及晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“在 DFT 能量/力/应力之外加入跨温度构型、相对自由能、熵或热膨胀信息；用主动学习补采相变、畴壁和相竞争区域，再用热力学积分、MC 或有限温度 MD 验证”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在HfO₂ 基铁电、二维滑移铁电、钙钛矿和磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“在 DFT 能量/力/应力之外加入跨温度构型、相对自由能、熵或热膨胀信息；用主动学习补采相变、畴壁和相竞争区域，再用热力学积分、MC 或有限温度 MD 验证”补充训练/验证集，再比较《广义标准材料框架内的数据驱动内聚区建模》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    文献研究：SeqMaestro: From nucleotide sequences to biological hypotheses through interpretable machine learning#
    SeqMaestro: From nucleotide sequences to biological hypotheses through interpretable machine learning#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · AI×化学·材料📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Evgeny S. Saveliev, Krzysztof Kacprzyk, Charlotte Capitanchik, Neelanjan Mukherjee, Kate Matlin, Ryan Sheridan 等10位作者
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.14882v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Nucleotide sequence analysis is central to problems spanning regulatory genomics, evolutionary biology, and phenotype prediction. Classical bioinformatics methods extract interpretable sequence properties such as motifs and k-mer composition, but their flexibility is limited. In contrast, modern deep learning models can learn powerful predictive representations directly from raw sequences, yet their internal representations and decision mechanisms are difficult to inspect. Interpretable machine learning methods (e.g., sparse linear models and decision trees) provide human-understandable representations of predictive relationships but are not designed to operate directly on nucleotide sequences. Here, we introduce SeqMaestro, a machine learning framework that proposes biological hypotheses from nucleotide sequences using interpretable models. Our solution is centered around a two-layer interface that connects nucleotide sequences with the broader ecosystem of interpretable machine learning. SeqMaestro uses this interface to fit diverse combinations of interpretable models, feature representations, and extraction strategies, leveraging variability across transparent models to identify robust biological signals and richer predictive relationships than feature importance alone can provide. The system also supports data transformation and cleaning, model fitting, hyperparameter tuning, reliability analysis, and synthesis of results into a contextualized written report. By providing these capabilities through a no-code workflow, SeqMaestro is designed to make interpretable sequence analysis accessible to researchers without requiring extensive programming or machine learning expertise. SeqMaestro thereby provides an accessible route from nucleotide sequences to biological hypotheses.

    Physics Informed Neural Network model for the dynamical study of Abdominal Aortic Aneurysm#
    🧭 方法 / 工具P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Adrián Robles Arques, Martín Ruiz Fernandez, Javier Sanchis, Miguel A. Teruel, Juan Trujillo

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.15104v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We present the development and application of a three-dimensional Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) framework for the investigation of haemodynamic behaviour in the human aorta. The model incorporates a time-resolved simulation of pulsatile blood flow over a two-minute interval, enabling the extraction of pressure and velocity fields with high temporal fidelity. The mechanical stress exerted on the aortic wall was quantified through Laplace's law, with temporal averaging applied to derive representative stress distributions. This approach circumvents the computational overhead associated with conventional computational fluid dynamics (CFD) methods by eliminating mesh generation and exploiting the automatic differentiation capabilities inherent to neural networks. The proposed methodology demonstrates that PINNs can serve as an efficient and accurate alternative for modelling complex vascular flow phenomena, offering significant advantages in scalability and computational cost reduction while maintaining physical consistency.

    文献研究：Physics-Informed Neural Network for Stability Analysis of Embankments Supported by GESCs with Bending Deformation#
    Physics-Informed Neural Network for Stability Analysis of Embankments Supported by GESCs with Bending Deformation#
    🧭 方法 / 工具P3 · AI×物理📖 Preprints👤 Xiaocong Cai, Ling Zhang, Bocheng Peng, Jingpeng Tan, Zijian Yang
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    The stability assessment of embankments on soft soils improved with geosynthetic-encased stone columns (GESCs) is complex, particularly when considering the bending deformation of columns under seismic loading. This study proposes a novel physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework, integrating a conventional machine learning model with a physical constraint (column bending deformation) to reformulate the loss function for enhanced prediction accuracy. A high-quality dataset is generated through the proposed and validated theoretical method that incorporates the bending moment contribution of GESCs. A set of specific equations accounting for the bending deformation of the column is derived and directly integrated into the PINN model as a physical law. The trained PINN successfully learns the complex mapping from 15 input geotechnical and geometric parameters to critical outputs of the factor of safety (FS), the critical bending moment (Mu), compressive strength (fc), and the compression central angle (α). Validation and test results demonstrate exceptional predictive accuracy, with coefficients of determination exceeding 99%. Furthermore, a global sensitivity analysis quantifies the direct mutual influence of each input parameter. The developed PINN model can rapidly achieve the probabilistic stability assessment and optimized design of GESC-supported embankments.

    文献研究：HiGFRL: Hierarchical Graph Fusion-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Dependency-Aware Task Scheduling in Heterogeneous Cloud#
    HiGFRL: Hierarchical Graph Fusion-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Dependency-Aware Task Scheduling in Heterogeneous Cloud#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · 拓扑·电子结构📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Tiangang Li, Shi Ying, Xiangbo Tian
    相关度
    7
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.14968v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Online scheduling of dependency-aware tasks in heterogeneous cloud clusters is a fundamental yet challenging problem due to the complex interplay between DAG topologies and multi-dimensional resource constraints. While DRL has shown promise, existing GNN-based approaches often struggle to efficiently model high-order topological dependencies and suffer from loose coupling between task and resource states, leading to myopic scheduling decisions. To address these limitations, we propose HiGFRL, a Hierarchical Graph Fusion-Driven Reinforcement Learning framework. HiGFRL constructs a novel three-level state representation comprising a Static Hypergraph, a Dynamic Global Graph, and a Local Bipartite Graph to explicitly model the interplay between task dependencies and real-time cluster dynamics. Specifically, we design a fusion-driven dual-network architecture to optimize RL decision-making, where a Context Fusion Allocator integrates local bipartite matching features with fused global context to execute precise task-to-node allocation, and a Global State Evaluator leverages the global dynamic graph representation to accurately estimate expected long-term cumulative reward. Furthermore, we incorporate a topology-prior-guided hybrid reward mechanism that distills static topological priors into the learning process to accelerate convergence. Extensive experiments using real-world Alibaba cluster traces demonstrate that HiGFRL significantly outperforms heuristics and DRL baselines. Specifically, in challenging large-scale high-load scenarios, HiGFRL reduces the Makespan by up to 32.55%, and optimizes the average task flow time and average task wait time by 13.58% and 13.79%, respectively. Experimental results confirm that HiGFRL not only significantly improves cluster throughput but also ensures superior QoS by substantially reducing queuing delays. Code Release:https://github.com/igeng/HiGFRL.

    文献研究：Nonparametric Variance-Penalized Actor-Critic: Statistical Inference for Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning#
    Nonparametric Variance-Penalized Actor-Critic: Statistical Inference for Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · 超导📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Saunak Kumar Panda, Tong Li, Yisha Xiang, Ruiqi Liu
    相关度
    7
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.14327v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Variance penalization is a principled approach to risk-sensitive reinforcement learning (RL) that explicitly trades expected return for policy stability. Existing methods require a dedicated second critic to estimate return variance online, adding architectural complexity and compounding estimation error during learning. We propose a nonparametric variance-penalized actor-critic (VPAC) framework that replaces the variance critic with statistically grounded online estimators based on bootstrapping and random scaling, techniques drawn from the statistical inference literature for stochastic approximation. These estimators require no auxiliary network, maintain a single-critic architecture, and produce variance penalties that are bounded by construction, enabling clean convergence analysis. We establish almost-sure convergence for both a variance-penalized Q-learning algorithm and a two-timescale actor-critic variant via the ordinary differential equation (ODE) method, requiring only that variance estimates remain bounded rather than consistent. Empirically, we evaluate across discrete and continuous stochastic environments, demonstrating that the proposed methods match or exceed the variance reduction achieved by the existing dual-critic VPAC baseline while eliminating the overhead of a second critic. We further validate on a high-temperature superconductor (HTS) manufacturing case study, where VPAC-RS (Random Scaling) achieves a 74% reduction in steady-state critical current variability and a 63% reduction in episode return standard deviation, translating directly to improved yield consistency. Our results establish nonparametric statistical inference as a practical and theoretically sound alternative to auxiliary critics for risk-sensitive RL.

    💡 亮点：arXiv:2609.14327v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Variance penalization is a principled approach to risk-sensitive reinforcement learning (RL) that explicitly trades expected return for policy stability…

    文献研究：Programming Spintronic Reservoir Computing#
    Programming Spintronic Reservoir Computing#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / physics👤 Yuichiro Terasaki, Yusuke Imai, Jason Z. Kim, Kohei Nakajima
    相关度
    7
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13169v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: We present a programming framework for a spintronic reservoir computer (RC) that maps prescribed input-output relationships directly onto the readout layer, bypassing conventional data-driven black-box approaches. Our spintronic RC is based on magnetoresistive random-access memory and exploits magnetization dynamics for computation. We introduce a general metric that quantifies the system's programmability and reveals how the governing equations and system parameters constrain the class of realizable functions. We then construct externally controllable readout layers by exploiting the explicit parameter dependence of the prescribed equations. This metric and construction enable programming explicit functions on the spintronic RC, indicating a potential route to in-memory computing. Our demonstrations include neural-network emulation, bifurcation embedding, and a Newton solver for fifth-order algebraic equations. In addition, we prove the universal approximation property of the spintronic RC in the limit of infinite system size and input duration, and show its consistency with programmability.

    利用深度学习预测恒星条件下等离子体的不透明度#
    Opacity predictions in plasmas under stellar conditions using deep learning#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / physics👤 Djamel Benredjem, Jean-Christophe Pain
    相关度
    0

    📄 摘要：本研究旨在预测恒星条件下等离子体的不透明度。作者重点研究铁和镍，因为这两种元素在理论和实验方面都得到了广泛研究。在某些状态下，尤其是在非局域热力学平衡条件下，计算光谱不透明度的计算代价可能很高。为减轻这一困难，作者采用深度学习模型，在大幅降低计算成本的同时预测准确的不透明度。具体而言，作者开发并训练了一种结合卷积神经网络和多层感知器的混合模型。所提出的方法能够给出0至10000 eV能量范围内的光谱不透明度，以及大量温度和质量密度条件下相应的平均不透明度，并且与标准计算方法高度一致。除高温镍在L壳层和M壳层能区的情况外，整体吻合度非常令人满意；在这些区域，大量新出现的跃迁导致了显著差异。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13252v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The aim of this work is to predict the opacity of plasmas under stellar conditions. We focus on iron and nickel, as these elements have been extensively investigated both theoretically and experimentally. In certain regimes, notably under non-local thermodynamic equilibrium, calculating the spectral opacity can be computationally demanding. To mitigate this difficulty, we employ deep learning models to predict accurate opacities while achieving a substantial reduction in computational cost. Specifically, we develop and train a hybrid model that combines a convolutional neural network with a multilayer perceptron. The proposed approach provides both the spectral opacity over the 0--10,000 eV energy range, and the corresponding mean opacities for large sets of temperatures and mass densities, in close agreement with standard computational methods. The agreement is very satisfactory, except in the L- and M-shell energy ranges for nickel at high temperatures, where a very large number of emerging transitions leads to substantial discrepancies.

    💡 亮点：本文旨在预测恒星条件下等离子体的不透明度，重点研究铁和镍。作者构建并训练了卷积神经网络与多层感知器相结合的混合模型，用于预测0至10000 eV能量范围内的光谱不透明度及相应的平均不透明度。结果与标准计算方法总体符合良好，同时显著降低了计算成本；但在高温镍等离子体的L壳层和M壳层能区，由于大量跃迁产生，仍存在较大偏差。

    文献研究：A Machine Learning API for Earth Observation Data Cubes Based on openEO#
    A Machine Learning API for Earth Observation Data Cubes Based on openEO#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Brian Pondi, Jonas Hurst, Rolf Simoes, Jonas Starke, Marius Appel, Edzer Pebesma
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13453v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) data are increasingly organized as spatio-temporal data cubes, while machine learning (ML) methods operate on tabular feature matrices or structured tensor inputs. This mismatch forces platform-specific transformations that are difficult to reproduce or transfer across cloud infrastructures. The openEO specification provides a unified interface for EO data access and processing across heterogeneous backends, but lacks a standardized approach for ML integration. We propose a process-level ML specification for openEO structured into three stages: model initialization, model actions (training, tuning, inference, validation), and model management. It supports classical algorithms such as Random Forest and SVM, as well as deep learning architectures for time-series and spatial patch-based modeling, including TempCNN, Temporal Attention Encoders, and foundation model inference. Three prototype implementations in R and Python demonstrate feasibility across diverse technology stacks. A crop type mapping use case demonstrates cross-backend interoperability by submitting an identical process graph to independent R and Python backends and comparing predictions and evaluation metrics. Two further use cases demonstrate deep learning on time series and foundation model inference, each executed on a dedicated backend. The prototypes reveal, however, that full cross-backend portability requires deeper harmonization of serialization formats and execution semantics than the process level alone can enforce; backend library versions and preprocessing conventions outside the specification's boundary also affect reproducibility. Addressing both through explicit backend conformance profiles represents the most important near-term direction. The specification advances the reproducibility, portability, and accessibility of ML workflows on EO data cubes across cloud platforms.
